- <a id="change-696484177896400"></a>**[cdm:#court-report](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-report)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-696484177896400)
  - Added annotation on [cdm:#court-report](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-report): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "The Reports of Cases before the Court of Justice and the General Court. The Reports of Cases before the Court of Justice and the General Court, generally known as Court Reports, publishes the collections of documents that belong to one case (e.g. Summary of the Judgment, Opinion of Advocate General and Judgment of the Court). Structually, one publication consists of: - the front matter including the index of subject matter and the table of contents - a series of cases, each in turn composed of the individual documents Some of these documents are already part of sector 6 (e.g. Opinion of Advocate General and Judgment of the Court), others not, e.g. the front matter and the summary. The CDM must therefore model some parts of the court documents as well. To make things more complicated, the summary of the case is in the Court Report separated from the judgement and introduces the complete case (e.g. ECR 2010-1, p. 1f) and joined to the judgement itself in EUR-Lex (e.g. celex 62008CJ0229). A number of key properties such as catalogue number and ISSN are modelled on the manifestation level as they can vary according to the type of manifestation chosen (e.g. print vs. eBook). One number (= fascicle) of the Court Report can be split over multiple volumes that are conventionally numbered with Latin capital letters, e.g. "2010 - 1 (A)" and "2010 - 1 (B)". They are consecutively numbered so that "2010 - 1 (A)" ends with page 486 and "2010 - 1 (B)" starts with page 487. Only the first volume has the front-matter (except for the title page). The CDM therefore treats these volumes as part of one work and only distinguishes the parts of the full Court Report at the item level (i.e. as parts of the various manifestation of the full Court Report). This is, however, probably not in line with the current practice in EU-Bookshop. However, as long as the partial catalogue numbers are stored, the Court Reports would be finable both by the "global catalogue number" that is manifestation wide (i.e. for one manifestation of the complete fascicle) and by a catalogue number essentially on item level. The page numbers that an legal resource appears on are part of the legal resource's print publication. So, an individual case law expression will be linked to a manifestation_print that in turn is lnked to the work of the Court Report."^^[xsd:string](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#string)
- <a id="change-696484178587500"></a>**[cdm:#court-type](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-type)** `Add Annotation to Entity` [permalink](#change-696484178587500)
  - Added annotation on [cdm:#court-type](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-type): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "The type of Court handling a case (concepts from AT court-type)"^^[xsd:string](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#string)

- <a id="change-696484177402700"></a>**[cdm:#court-report](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-report)** `Remove Annotation from Entity` [permalink](#change-696484177402700)
  - Removed annotation from [cdm:#court-report](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-report): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "The Reports of Cases before the Court of Justice and the General Court. The Reports of Cases before the Court of Justice and the General Court, generally known as Court Reports, publishes the collections of documents that belong to one case (e.g. Summary of the Judgment, Opinion of Advocate General and Judgment of the Court). Structually, one publication consists of: - the front matter including the index of subject matter and the table of contents - a series of cases, each in turn composed of the individual documents Some of these documents are already part of sector 6 (e.g. Opinion of Advocate General and Judgment of the Court), others not, e.g. the front matter and the summary. The CDM must therefore model some parts of the court documents as well. To make things more complicated, the summary of the case is in the Court Report separated from the judgement and introduces the complete case (e.g. ECR 2010-1, p. 1f) and joined to the judgement itself in EUR-Lex (e.g. celex 62008CJ0229). A number of key properties such as catalogue number and issn are modelled on the manifestation level as they can vary according to the type of manifestation chosen (e.g. print vs. eBook). One number (= fascicle) of the Court Report can be split over multiple volumes that are conventionally numbered with Latin capital letters, e.g. "2010 - 1 (A)" and "2010 - 1 (B)". They are consecutively numbered so that "2010 - 1 (A)" ends with page 486 and "2010 - 1 (B)" starts with page 487. Only the first volume has the front-matter (except for the title page). The CDM therefore treats these volumes as part of one work and only distinguishes the parts of the full Court Report at the item level (i.e. as parts of the various manifestation of the full Court Report). This is, however, probably not in line with the current practice in EU-Bookshop. However, as long as the partial catalogue numbers are stored, the Court Reports would be finable both by the "global catalogue number" that is manifestation wide (i.e. for one manifestation of the complete fascicle) and by a catalogue number essentially on item level. The page numbers that an legal resource appears on are part of the legal resource's print publication. So, an individual case law expression will be linked to a manifestation_print that in turn is lnked to the work of the Court Report."^^[xsd:string](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#string)
- <a id="change-696484178266500"></a>**[cdm:#court-type](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-type)** `Remove Annotation from Entity` [permalink](#change-696484178266500)
  - Removed annotation from [cdm:#court-type](http://publications.europa.eu/ontology/cdm#court-type): [rdfs:comment](http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#comment) = "The type of Court handling a case"^^[xsd:string](http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#string)
